Welcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a ***Magic** 2012* draft from a Last Chance Qualifier for the [**Magic Online** Championship Series](https://web.archive.org/web/20111012000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/other/12132010c).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da818_thumb.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20111012000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=2890930)
